- **ContestHQ**: Runs a level 3-5 contest end to end inside its folder: writes RUBRIC/SPEC/PLAN/TASKS from the harness templates, runs the score loop (ContestJudge predicts plan scores for 2-3 candidates and the plan is improved before building, then draft and final scores, each recorded with `ach score`), gathers evidence with DomainResearcher/ArticleResearcher, decides what to reuse instead of build with OpenSourceScout whenever the deliverable includes code, a model, a service, an experiment pipeline, or data tooling (and production references with ReferenceScout at level 5), dispatches worker waves sized… ([FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness](https://github.com/FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness/blob/HEAD/agents/ContestHQ.md))
- **ContestJudge**: Strict contest reviewer. Stage `plan` predicts the score a concept/SPEC/PLAN would earn if completed as planned, judged against this contest's organizer/host, purpose and judging criteria (reported as `basis`), and proposes per-criterion plan changes that raise it; stages `draft` and `final` run gated compliance checks (format, limits, eligibility, AI disclosure, rule violations) first, map every notice rule and judging criterion to where the deliverable satisfies it, then score each `.harness/gate.json` criterion. Returns stage, total, target, verdict (ready | revise | reject), per-criterion… ([FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness](https://github.com/FMsongX2/Autocontest-Harness/blob/HEAD/agents/ContestJudge.md))
- **ContestScout**: Finds new contests that the 11 `ach collect` sources (linkareer, wevity, thinkcontest, allcon, contestkorea, detizen, campuspick, allforyoung, dacon, devpost, kaggle) do not list — government, local-government and public-agency notice boards, academic societies, companies' own contests, overseas institutions — via recency web search, verifies each on its official page, writes survivors to a JSON file and registers them with `ach add --from-file` (which runs the duplicate check per item).
